# 2. Interface contract table

### Full seed to asymptotic negative resolution
**Input required.** The exact original h,Lambda,M of Section0.

**Input required.** Original all-split benchmark,integer rays Z_b,full

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "Arbitrary many or variable steps imply arbitrary words."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.** The actual optimized policy has a
  complete constant-step obstruction and an exact unit-step boundary.
- "Joint covariance can be discarded without cost."

  induction failure. A new block identity must retain and price it.
- "Positive entries plus stable decimation give PSD."
  **Mathematically incorrect as a general inference.**ff8f8f21b222a81b
  leaves signed margins and the constant load unresolved on the actual word.
- "Second-step exclusion also excludes the reversed region."
  **Not automatic.** No primitive reversal or lossless ordering result is available.
- "The independent scalar audit certifies every global reduction."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.** It explicitly excludes named
  Bernstein/global arithmetic and requires a local repair.

Do not repeat optimal-height tuning, closed safe-angle tuning, positivity-
only kernel arguments, finite-field-to-real-PSD leaps, unaudited domain
reductions or no-seed-to-affirmative-target inference.

B_(2^m-1)-scale payoff genuinely improving the complete reference gap;

1252b8823bb5a290 and the accepted reference facts.
**Missing checks.** Explicit growing-dimensional or multistage law,

subsequent arbitrary-word/horizon bridge. The finite dimension66 is

of the genuinely new law;another weak common-coordinate/bounded-prefix

**Target statement.** An actual r>=5 h,Lambda,M satisfying the central

length-specific threshold; any claimed extension requires proof.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed facts retain exactly their accepted scopes:

# 2. Interface contract table

### Full primitive seed to original asymptotic separation
**Input required.** Every original hypothesis in Section0,at actual
sufficient capacity,including the full matrix space and true physical word.
**Output claimed.** Fixed-relative all-function improvement on the exact
unbounded physical horizons stated in the accepted facts.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 and4c92d3fc4eb6153e.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The actual improving seed.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A conditional theorem becomes an unsupported
negative answer,or a numerical matrix is mistaken for an exact certificate.
**Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### Equality chart and strict point to a sufficient endpoint
**Input required.** Original T,L,omega,D,M;all signs and full PSD;
chart potential z=T e with valid tree denominators or exact compatibility.
**Output claimed.** A coordinate representation and a candidate start.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750's coefficient identity.

endpoint. The new chart/density statements themselves remain research drafts.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Equality enforcement,an interior point or a
successful factorization is advertised as threshold crossing or global optimum.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Actual function/state laws to useful terminal error
**Input required.** One concrete static function,all actual queries,
proved smoothness and minimum,paid initial distance and chronological state.
**Output claimed.** A lower score at original B_n scale on unbounded H_m.
**Available facts.**1252b8823bb5a290 and complete reference facts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Gibbs terminal entropy or
centered-simplex full-history residual/support control.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Global function legality,early face changes,
finite samples or a state identity replace the missing terminal estimate.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Arbitrary primitive template to recursively feasible physical inflation
**Input required.** Actual template and child certificates with every
original sign and full PSD;nonnegative physical steps at every level.
**Output claimed.** Capacity multiplication on the specified inserted word.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750/4c92d3fc4eb6153e only establish their
stated basic-tree use of arbitrary seeds;the roles are not interchangeable.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Collective block interpolation
and full-array transformation for an arbitrary template.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A source exponent lower bound is used to
prune finite seeds without a valid recursive algorithm/certificate.
**Status.** UNKNOWN.

### Diagnostic-family lower bound to every original word
**Input required.** A schedule-independent threshold N_epsilon and an
actual static-function construction for every n and every h>=0.
**Output claimed.** The full affirmative statement in Section0.
**Available facts.**Only generic interpolation and scoped lower witnesses.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The entire arbitrary-word
extension,even after a hypothetical useful H_m result.
**Failure mode if ignored.** One family,dyadic horizons or an h-dependent
error is substituted for the original minimax quantifiers.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "A feasible primitive child is a smooth-convex virtual oracle."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.** The explicit draft counterexample
  b53b38b27498e8a0 invalidates that premise while its physical lift remains
  valid in the basic case. A collective certificate is a different obligation.
- "The known exponent bounds every finite primitive capacity."
  **Conditional only.** Such an inference needs an actual recursive
  nonnegative-step construction with uniform full-class guarantees. The
  arbitrary-template closure is not supplied by the accepted basic-tree facts.
- "Equal total child clocks give equal endpoints."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**1ad14d90628c1312 retains a
  nonlinear chronological dependence and an explicit counterexample.
- "A preimage exists,so its initial radius is competitive."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.**d23006aab2b6e9be proves existence
  but the selected norm bounds are weak;long steps need not be injective.
- "Simple probability thresholding gives the Haar-body projection."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**aa3c5e1d91baf405 retains the
  true non-isometric metric and actual changing supports.
- "An initialization/serialization failure disproves feasibility."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.** Without a usable original array
  or an actual optimizer run there is no mathematical conclusion to draw.

Do not reopen a named closed family through an unchanged parameter retuning.
Do not infer all-ellipsoid,all-tree,all-scalar or full-cone impossibility.
Do not replace the complete comparison by only one convenient reference.
Do not identify zero-step formal gradients in the primitive certificate.
Do not transfer a finite H6 object to a different enclosing-m child.
Do not label the all-word uniformity or the missing chronological payment routine.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1 - actual sufficient-capacity full seed
**Target statement.** The exact existential coefficient statement of Section0.
**Prerequisites.** One actual nonnegative word and explicit full original array.
**Existing facts to use.**04219554e41d9750 and4c92d3fc4eb6153e.
**Missing checks.** All original signs,flow and full PSD at the required capacity.
**Closure criterion.** A complete exact object survives independent attack
and verifier acceptance,then matches the accepted physical lift. Independent
of the positive-function route.

### Bridge2 - actual terminal estimate for the specified growing bodies
**Target statement.** For a specified legal tree body and original H_m,
prove a quantitative useful 2(F(x_n)-F_star)/(L R^2) at exact B_n scale
for every sufficiently large m,or prove the chosen object asymptotically weak.
The Gibbs candidate is F=log(2^-m sum exp(<a_s,x>)),with tree weights
w_j=(2/c_m)^j/sum_(k<m)(2/c_m)^k and its specified paid initial state.
The centered projection candidate is F_K with K the convex hull of those
leaf vectors and a leaf start;the draft gives L=1,minimum0 and R=1.
All claimed preliminary formulas must be proved for the actual object.
**Prerequisites.** Full chronological GD,one static function,true minimum
and distance;no fresh-child clocks or permanent-core assumption.
**Existing facts to use.**1252b8823bb5a290 and scope-correct reference facts.
**Missing checks.** The actual terminal entropy/normal-defect inequality;
function definitions and first-query formulas do not settle it.
**Closure criterion.** A readable uniform trajectory/payoff argument,not
finite numerical agreement or an unpriced global state. Independent of Bridge1.

### Bridge3 - decide arbitrary-template inflation on its actual scope
**Target statement.** Given primitive template h with h_i>=1 and primitive
child q of capacity C,prove or refute full primitive feasibility of
q,1+C(h0-1),q,...,1+C(h_(r-1)-1),q at capacity C(1+sum h).
**Prerequisites.** Original input certificates,physical counts and one
underlying function;proper subclasses and singular cases must be explicit.
**Existing facts to use.**The accepted basic-tree substitution is a scoped
starting point only,not this arbitrary-template conclusion.
**Missing checks.** A genuine nonnegative full-array lift/PSD proof or an
actual physical counterexample;failure of one array formula is insufficient.
**Closure criterion.** A joint theorem or counterexample with its exact
domain. Any exponent consequence also requires checking the external
Ye-Liu theorem and all recursive hypotheses. Independent of Bridge2;
it can constrain Bridge1 only after the needed closure is established.

Even a complete Bridge2 result leaves the original positive theorem's
every-n/every-h schedule-independent requirement. That is the original
load-bearing mathematical task,not an auxiliary lemma already almost proved.

# 2. Interface contract table

**Input required.**In draftcdd7f057606aeb18,0<kappa<=epsilon<=1/2,Theta=atan(1/epsilon),

1/[2(1+s_tail)]*product_i[b_i/(c_sp*(a_sp+s_i)+epsilon*b_i)]^2.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "Local certificate capacity optimality proves objective minimax optimality."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.**a23356adcbb96c7e has a specified
  neighborhood and coefficient class;it does not optimize original W_n.
- "The new smaller activation constant removes the profile loss."
  **Conditional only.**It remains exponential in the matched small parameter,
  while transfer and threshold losses accumulate over the checkpoint count.
- "Use epsilon<kappa in the already proved formula."
  **Not automatic.**Coordinate extrema,normals,global planes and the true
  radius must be re-established with any changed constants.
- "A good record-level selection optimizes every checkpoint selection."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.**A selected pattern gives a lower
  certificate only;the maximum over all patterns needs an additional argument.
- "A weak mass-only certificate upper-bounds the constructed function."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**The certificate is a lower estimate;
  its failure need not refute the actual true-distance payoff.
- "A killed reward is controlled by its killing vertex or pathwise budget."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**20e54647c50a5a7f gives exact original
  arrays with missing signed compensation.

Do not assume arbitrary-template inflation from basic-tree substitution.
Do not delete independent zero-step formal coordinates in seed certificates.
Do not reset old function values or nonlinear child states.
Do not treat a source paper's symbol R as project R without normalization.
Do not transfer finite H6 or a different enclosing parameter into another word.
Do not claim a particular finite word lies in an existential local radius.
Do not promote finite exact arithmetic readback to a full asymptotic theorem.

**Target statement.**The complete finite-word lemma and both precise lower

**Target statement.**For each delta>0,find N_delta independent of h such

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "Arbitrary many or variable steps imply arbitrary words."

  **Conflated with a stronger claim.** It explicitly excludes named
  Bernstein/global arithmetic and requires a local repair.

Do not repeat optimal-height tuning, closed safe-angle tuning, positivity-
only kernel arguments, finite-field-to-real-PSD leaps, unaudited domain
reductions or no-seed-to-affirmative-target inference.

full seed. Independent of all positive construction mechanisms.

### Bridge2 - useful nonminimum or dependent state
**Target statement.** Construct a static continuation outside the closed
policy, retaining all physical rows and true radius, and decide its full
declared-family payoff on original mixed blocks or H_m.

### Bridge3 - genuine joint-block covariance identity
**Target statement.** A uniform exact decomposition/control of the full
stated chronological residual kernel, including constant and final
columns, or a precise exact counterexample to the proposed decomposition.

killing-step terms; block dimension and any growth cost explicit.

negative directions still require independent all-Q legality assessment.

### Bridge4 - obtuse endpoint quantitative comparison

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

# 2. Interface contract table

### Full primitive seed to original asymptotic separation
**Input required.** Every original hypothesis in Section0,at actual
sufficient capacity,including the full matrix space and true physical word.
**Output claimed.** Fixed-relative all-function improvement on the exact
unbounded physical horizons stated in the accepted facts.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 and4c92d3fc4eb6153e.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The actual improving seed.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A conditional theorem becomes an unsupported
negative answer,or a numerical matrix is mistaken for an exact certificate.
**Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### Equality chart and strict point to a sufficient endpoint
**Input required.** Original T,L,omega,D,M;all signs and full PSD;
chart potential z=T e with valid tree denominators or exact compatibility.
**Output claimed.** A coordinate representation and a candidate start.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750's coefficient identity.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Exact sufficient-capacity
endpoint. The new chart/density statements themselves remain research drafts.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Equality enforcement,an interior point or a
successful factorization is advertised as threshold crossing or global optimum.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Actual function/state laws to useful terminal error
**Input required.** One concrete static function,all actual queries,
proved smoothness and minimum,paid initial distance and chronological state.
**Output claimed.** A lower score at original B_n scale on unbounded H_m.
**Available facts.**1252b8823bb5a290 and complete reference facts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Gibbs terminal entropy or
centered-simplex full-history residual/support control.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Global function legality,early face changes,
finite samples or a state identity replace the missing terminal estimate.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Arbitrary primitive template to recursively feasible physical inflation
**Input required.** Actual template and child certificates with every
original sign and full PSD;nonnegative physical steps at every level.
**Output claimed.** Capacity multiplication on the specified inserted word.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750/4c92d3fc4eb6153e only establish their
stated basic-tree use of arbitrary seeds;the roles are not interchangeable.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Collective block interpolation
and full-array transformation for an arbitrary template.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A source exponent lower bound is used to
prune finite seeds without a valid recursive algorithm/certificate.
**Status.** UNKNOWN.

### Diagnostic-family lower bound to every original word
**Input required.** A schedule-independent threshold N_epsilon and an
actual static-function construction for every n and every h>=0.
**Output claimed.** The full affirmative statement in Section0.
**Available facts.**Only generic interpolation and scoped lower witnesses.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The entire arbitrary-word
extension,even after a hypothetical useful H_m result.
**Failure mode if ignored.** One family,dyadic horizons or an h-dependent
error is substituted for the original minimax quantifiers.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Finite phase product to an actual selected physical ray
**Input required.** A full primitive seed as in Section0,integer m=r+1,
K,ell>=1 with m^K<=2^ell,A^K>lambda^ell,and the actual accepted integer
ray limits Z_b. Every original sign,flow and full-space PSD remains.
**Output claimed.** Candidate0fc70796cd27f96d claims a finite phase choice,
certified depth and a fixed-relative improvement along its true physical
lengths. It also states the exact rational sufficient floors above.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 supplies the ray estimates and actual
basic-tree seed substitution;4c92d3fc4eb6153e supplies a narrower accepted
fixed-ray alternative. Neither supplies an actual improving seed.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Product direction,full
hypothesis matching,finite selection and every advertised exact corollary
in the new complete candidate;then the actual input seed.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A favorable product or existence of a phase
is mistaken for a certified physical algorithm,or an unaudited conditional
threshold is treated as the negative solution.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Full PSD image to nonnegative original multipliers
**Input required.** The original T,L,D,flow and all r(r+1) entries,with
nonnegative h. Nonzero,zero-chi,singular and all-zero cases are distinct.
**Output claimed.** Draftff8b941536cb035e gives joint exact symmetric/skew
coordinates and a Gram/Schur parametrization of the full PSD image.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 supplies the original primitive identity.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The complete new representation
and all boundaries;more decisively,an actual point whose reconstructed
original multipliers are all nonnegative at sufficient capacity.
**Failure mode if ignored.** PSD feasibility before the coefficient signs
is silently identified with full primitive feasibility. Me=0 is imposed
on the flow-only image without justification.
**Status.** PARTIAL.
# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "A feasible primitive child is a smooth-convex virtual oracle."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.** The explicit draft counterexample
  b53b38b27498e8a0 invalidates that premise while its physical lift remains
  valid in the basic case. A collective certificate is a different obligation.
- "The known exponent bounds every finite primitive capacity."
  **Conditional only.** Such an inference needs an actual recursive
  nonnegative-step construction with uniform full-class guarantees. The
  arbitrary-template closure is not supplied by the accepted basic-tree facts.
- "Equal total child clocks give equal endpoints."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**1ad14d90628c1312 retains a
  nonlinear chronological dependence and an explicit counterexample.
- "A preimage exists,so its initial radius is competitive."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.**d23006aab2b6e9be proves existence
  but the selected norm bounds are weak;long steps need not be injective.
- "Simple probability thresholding gives the Haar-body projection."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**aa3c5e1d91baf405 retains the
  true non-isometric metric and actual changing supports.
- "An initialization/serialization failure disproves feasibility."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.** Without a usable original array
  or an actual optimizer run there is no mathematical conclusion to draw.

Do not reopen a named closed family through an unchanged parameter retuning.
Do not infer all-ellipsoid,all-tree,all-scalar or full-cone impossibility.
Do not replace the complete comparison by only one convenient reference.
Do not identify zero-step formal gradients in the primitive certificate.
Do not transfer a finite H6 object to a different enclosing-m child.
Do not label the all-word uniformity or the missing chronological payment routine.

- "The PSD slice already is a primitive certificate."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.**ff8b941536cb035e still retains every
  original coefficient sign. The free skew variables and PSD factorization
  are not a proof that those inequalities can be met.
- "Finite weak trajectories disprove the eventual lower law."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.**c93913a3af467ccc and0cd12494384b9c0f
  report finite numerical trajectories,not all-depth upper estimates.
# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1 - actual sufficient-capacity full seed
**Target statement.** The exact existential coefficient statement of Section0.
**Prerequisites.** One actual nonnegative word and explicit full original array.
**Existing facts to use.**04219554e41d9750 and4c92d3fc4eb6153e.
**Missing checks.** All original signs,flow and full PSD at the required capacity.
**Closure criterion.** A complete exact object survives independent attack
and verifier acceptance,then matches the accepted physical lift. Independent
of the positive-function route.

### Bridge2 - actual terminal estimate for the specified growing bodies
**Target statement.** For a specified legal tree body and original H_m,
prove a quantitative useful 2(F(x_n)-F_star)/(L R^2) at exact B_n scale
for every sufficiently large m,or prove the chosen object asymptotically weak.
The Gibbs candidate is F=log(2^-m sum exp(<a_s,x>)),with tree weights
w_j=(2/c_m)^j/sum_(k<m)(2/c_m)^k and its specified paid initial state.
The centered projection candidate is F_K with K the convex hull of those
leaf vectors and a leaf start;the draft gives L=1,minimum0 and R=1.
All claimed preliminary formulas must be proved for the actual object.
**Prerequisites.** Full chronological GD,one static function,true minimum
and distance;no fresh-child clocks or permanent-core assumption.
**Existing facts to use.**1252b8823bb5a290 and scope-correct reference facts.
**Missing checks.** The actual terminal entropy/normal-defect inequality;
function definitions and first-query formulas do not settle it.
**Closure criterion.** A readable uniform trajectory/payoff argument,not
finite numerical agreement or an unpriced global state. Independent of Bridge1.

### Bridge3 - decide arbitrary-template inflation on its actual scope
**Target statement.** Given primitive template h with h_i>=1 and primitive
child q of capacity C,prove or refute full primitive feasibility of
q,1+C(h0-1),q,...,1+C(h_(r-1)-1),q at capacity C(1+sum h).
**Prerequisites.** Original input certificates,physical counts and one
underlying function;proper subclasses and singular cases must be explicit.
**Existing facts to use.**The accepted basic-tree substitution is a scoped
starting point only,not this arbitrary-template conclusion.
**Missing checks.** A genuine nonnegative full-array lift/PSD proof or an
actual physical counterexample;failure of one array formula is insufficient.
**Closure criterion.** A joint theorem or counterexample with its exact
domain. Any exponent consequence also requires checking the external
Ye-Liu theorem and all recursive hypotheses. Independent of Bridge2;
it can constrain Bridge1 only after the needed closure is established.

Even a complete Bridge2 result leaves the original positive theorem's
every-n/every-h schedule-independent requirement. That is the original
load-bearing mathematical task,not an auxiliary lemma already almost proved.

### Bridge4 - complete finite phase-cover sufficient implication
**Target statement.** The full conditional statement of0fc70796cd27f96d:
from one original primitive seed of length r and capacity A,with integers
K,ell>=1 satisfying (r+1)^K<=2^ell and A^K>lambda^ell,select a finite
integer ray and certify a delta>0 and physical horizon sequence N_j->infinity
such that W_(N_j)^*<=(1-delta)/B_(N_j) for all sufficiently large j.
The original seed hypotheses and normalization are exactly those of
Section0. The three rational corollaries require their claimed margins.
**Prerequisites.** Accepted integer ray limits and actual basic-tree
substitution;no arbitrary-template closure and no imported finite capacity
ceiling from an exponent theorem.
**Existing facts to use.**04219554e41d9750,4c92d3fc4eb6153e at their stated scopes.
**Missing checks.** All candidate product,selection,offset and exact
constant arguments;an actual seed is an additional independent obligation.
**Closure criterion.** The whole conditional proof survives independent
attack and acceptance with explicit finite selection. It can lower the
sufficient target for Bridge1 but cannot replace Bridge1.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed target is W_n^*/U_n^ZJ->1,or a conclusive asymptotic separation.
Here x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i grad f(x_i)/L for a predetermined h>=0,
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2),W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h),
and W=2R. The affirmative statement is:every epsilon>0 admits a single
N_epsilon,independent of h,such that for ALL n>=N_epsilon and ALL
nonnegative words,W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ. All finite dimensions,
zero and arbitrarily large steps,true minimizers and actual last iterates
remain. A fixed-relative full-class upper improvement on unbounded
horizons suffices for a negative resolution.

Write lambda=1+sqrt2,C0=B0=1,
Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,
C_n=max_(k+l=n-1)Omega(C_k,C_l),
B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(C_k,B_l),U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.
The all-split recurrences,not a decimal leading coefficient,are the target.

The accepted primitive-seed amplification packages04219554e41d9750 and
4c92d3fc4eb6153e remain the most concrete negative architecture. Their
actual sufficient seed is absent. New full-equality coordinates and a
strict-interior starting certificate are research drafts below threshold.
The positive side now has several explicitly defined legal-function
drafts,but no useful benchmark-reaching unbounded-family witness and
no uniform arbitrary-word proof.

The new exclusions describe method failure,not proposition failure of
the original conjecture. Numerical non-discovery or a failed evaluator
initialization is not mathematical nonexistence. SUBSTANTIAL describes
an accepted implication with an unmatched hypothesis,not almost solved.

The complete UNVERIFIED phase-cover draft0fc70796cd27f96d proposes a
stronger sufficient seed interface using only integer ray limits and actual

linear algebra from positivity. For a nonzero nonnegative word,put
v=T^(-1)e and chi=1-A^2 v_r^2. It claims the real flow-only image is
M=M^T,e^T M e=0,v^T M v=chi. Only AFTER imposing PSD does Me=0 follow.
The resulting full PSD slice,skew reconstruction and boundary formulas
retain every original multiplier sign. This is a representation theorem
candidate,not a feasible sign array or a capacity increase.

The repaired equality-chart finite search actually optimized and retained
a best checked numerical A=9.694142701216034,below the stated sufficient
floors. Its tiny imaginary diagnostic eigenvalues do not prove infeasibility.
The centered-tree-simplex fixed-leaf numerical values at m6/8/10 were very
small,with an independent higher-precision agreement at m8;these finite
values do not prove eventual asymptotic failure. The mathematical evidence
currently supports method failure only,not proposition failure.

**Missing verification on the actual model.** Exact sufficient-capacity
endpoint. The new chart/density statements themselves remain research drafts.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Equality enforcement,an interior point or a
successful factorization is advertised as threshold crossing or global optimum.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Actual function/state laws to useful terminal error
**Input required.** One concrete static function,all actual queries,
proved smoothness and minimum,paid initial distance and chronological state.
**Output claimed.** A lower score at original B_n scale on unbounded H_m.
**Available facts.**1252b8823bb5a290 and complete reference facts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Gibbs terminal entropy or
centered-simplex full-history residual/support control.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Global function legality,early face changes,
finite samples or a state identity replace the missing terminal estimate.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Arbitrary primitive template to recursively feasible physical inflation
**Input required.** Actual template and child certificates with every
original sign and full PSD;nonnegative physical steps at every level.
**Output claimed.** Capacity multiplication on the specified inserted word.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750/4c92d3fc4eb6153e only establish their
stated basic-tree use of arbitrary seeds;the roles are not interchangeable.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Collective block interpolation
and full-array transformation for an arbitrary template.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A source exponent lower bound is used to
prune finite seeds without a valid recursive algorithm/certificate.
**Status.** UNKNOWN.

### Diagnostic-family lower bound to every original word
**Input required.** A schedule-independent threshold N_epsilon and an
actual static-function construction for every n and every h>=0.
**Output claimed.** The full affirmative statement in Section0.
**Available facts.**Only generic interpolation and scoped lower witnesses.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The entire arbitrary-word
extension,even after a hypothetical useful H_m result.
**Failure mode if ignored.** One family,dyadic horizons or an h-dependent
error is substituted for the original minimax quantifiers.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Finite phase product to an actual selected physical ray
**Input required.** A full primitive seed as in Section0,integer m=r+1,
K,ell>=1 with m^K<=2^ell,A^K>lambda^ell,and the actual accepted integer
ray limits Z_b. Every original sign,flow and full-space PSD remains.
**Output claimed.** Candidate0fc70796cd27f96d claims a finite phase choice,
certified depth and a fixed-relative improvement along its true physical
lengths. It also states the exact rational sufficient floors above.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 supplies the ray estimates and actual
basic-tree seed substitution;4c92d3fc4eb6153e supplies a narrower accepted
fixed-ray alternative. Neither supplies an actual improving seed.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Product direction,full
hypothesis matching,finite selection and every advertised exact corollary
in the new complete candidate;then the actual input seed.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A favorable product or existence of a phase
is mistaken for a certified physical algorithm,or an unaudited conditional
threshold is treated as the negative solution.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Full PSD image to nonnegative original multipliers
**Input required.** The original T,L,D,flow and all r(r+1) entries,with
nonnegative h. Nonzero,zero-chi,singular and all-zero cases are distinct.
**Output claimed.** Draftff8b941536cb035e gives joint exact symmetric/skew
coordinates and a Gram/Schur parametrization of the full PSD image.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 supplies the original primitive identity.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The complete new representation
and all boundaries;more decisively,an actual point whose reconstructed
original multipliers are all nonnegative at sufficient capacity.
**Failure mode if ignored.** PSD feasibility before the coefficient signs
is silently identified with full primitive feasibility. Me=0 is imposed
on the flow-only image without justification.
**Status.** PARTIAL.
# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "A feasible primitive child is a smooth-convex virtual oracle."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.** The explicit draft counterexample
  b53b38b27498e8a0 invalidates that premise while its physical lift remains
  valid in the basic case. A collective certificate is a different obligation.
- "The known exponent bounds every finite primitive capacity."
  **Conditional only.** Such an inference needs an actual recursive
  nonnegative-step construction with uniform full-class guarantees. The
  arbitrary-template closure is not supplied by the accepted basic-tree facts.
- "Equal total child clocks give equal endpoints."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**1ad14d90628c1312 retains a
  nonlinear chronological dependence and an explicit counterexample.
- "A preimage exists,so its initial radius is competitive."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.**d23006aab2b6e9be proves existence
  but the selected norm bounds are weak;long steps need not be injective.
- "Simple probability thresholding gives the Haar-body projection."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.**aa3c5e1d91baf405 retains the
  true non-isometric metric and actual changing supports.
- "An initialization/serialization failure disproves feasibility."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.** Without a usable original array
  or an actual optimizer run there is no mathematical conclusion to draw.

Do not reopen a named closed family through an unchanged parameter retuning.
Do not infer all-ellipsoid,all-tree,all-scalar or full-cone impossibility.
Do not replace the complete comparison by only one convenient reference.
Do not identify zero-step formal gradients in the primitive certificate.
Do not transfer a finite H6 object to a different enclosing-m child.
Do not label the all-word uniformity or the missing chronological payment routine.

- "The PSD slice already is a primitive certificate."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.**ff8b941536cb035e still retains every
  original coefficient sign. The free skew variables and PSD factorization
  are not a proof that those inequalities can be met.
- "Finite weak trajectories disprove the eventual lower law."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.**c93913a3af467ccc and0cd12494384b9c0f
  report finite numerical trajectories,not all-depth upper estimates.
# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1 - actual sufficient-capacity full seed
**Target statement.** The exact existential coefficient statement of Section0.
**Prerequisites.** One actual nonnegative word and explicit full original array.
**Existing facts to use.**04219554e41d9750 and4c92d3fc4eb6153e.
**Missing checks.** All original signs,flow and full PSD at the required capacity.
**Closure criterion.** A complete exact object survives independent attack
and verifier acceptance,then matches the accepted physical lift. Independent
of the positive-function route.

### Bridge2 - actual terminal estimate for the specified growing bodies
**Target statement.** For a specified legal tree body and original H_m,
prove a quantitative useful 2(F(x_n)-F_star)/(L R^2) at exact B_n scale
for every sufficiently large m,or prove the chosen object asymptotically weak.
The Gibbs candidate is F=log(2^-m sum exp(<a_s,x>)),with tree weights
w_j=(2/c_m)^j/sum_(k<m)(2/c_m)^k and its specified paid initial state.
The centered projection candidate is F_K with K the convex hull of those
leaf vectors and a leaf start;the draft gives L=1,minimum0 and R=1.
All claimed preliminary formulas must be proved for the actual object.
**Prerequisites.** Full chronological GD,one static function,true minimum
and distance;no fresh-child clocks or permanent-core assumption.
**Existing facts to use.**1252b8823bb5a290 and scope-correct reference facts.
**Missing checks.** The actual terminal entropy/normal-defect inequality;
function definitions and first-query formulas do not settle it.
**Closure criterion.** A readable uniform trajectory/payoff argument,not
finite numerical agreement or an unpriced global state. Independent of Bridge1.

### Bridge3 - decide arbitrary-template inflation on its actual scope
**Target statement.** Given primitive template h with h_i>=1 and primitive
child q of capacity C,prove or refute full primitive feasibility of
q,1+C(h0-1),q,...,1+C(h_(r-1)-1),q at capacity C(1+sum h).
**Prerequisites.** Original input certificates,physical counts and one
underlying function;proper subclasses and singular cases must be explicit.
**Existing facts to use.**The accepted basic-tree substitution is a scoped
starting point only,not this arbitrary-template conclusion.
**Missing checks.** A genuine nonnegative full-array lift/PSD proof or an
actual physical counterexample;failure of one array formula is insufficient.
**Closure criterion.** A joint theorem or counterexample with its exact
domain. Any exponent consequence also requires checking the external
Ye-Liu theorem and all recursive hypotheses. Independent of Bridge2;
it can constrain Bridge1 only after the needed closure is established.

Even a complete Bridge2 result leaves the original positive theorem's
every-n/every-h schedule-independent requirement. That is the original
load-bearing mathematical task,not an auxiliary lemma already almost proved.

### Bridge4 - complete finite phase-cover sufficient implication
**Target statement.** The full conditional statement of0fc70796cd27f96d:
from one original primitive seed of length r and capacity A,with integers
K,ell>=1 satisfying (r+1)^K<=2^ell and A^K>lambda^ell,select a finite
integer ray and certify a delta>0 and physical horizon sequence N_j->infinity
such that W_(N_j)^*<=(1-delta)/B_(N_j) for all sufficiently large j.
The original seed hypotheses and normalization are exactly those of
Section0. The three rational corollaries require their claimed margins.
**Prerequisites.** Accepted integer ray limits and actual basic-tree
substitution;no arbitrary-template closure and no imported finite capacity
ceiling from an exponent theorem.
**Existing facts to use.**04219554e41d9750,4c92d3fc4eb6153e at their stated scopes.
**Missing checks.** All candidate product,selection,offset and exact
constant arguments;an actual seed is an additional independent obligation.
**Closure criterion.** The whole conditional proof survives independent
attack and acceptance with explicit finite selection. It can lower the
sufficient target for Bridge1 but cannot replace Bridge1.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.** The exact original objective remains
lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a certified fixed relative full-class
upper improvement on an unbounded sequence of actual physical horizons.
For predetermined h in[0,infinity)^n, actual GD is
x_(t+1)=x_t-(h_t/L)grad f(x_t), and
W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2), W_n^*=inf_h W_n(h).
The supremum ranges over all finite dimensions, convex L-smooth functions
with an attained minimum, and all x0!=x_star. W=2R throughout.
The affirmative quantifiers are: for every delta>0 there is one N_delta
independent of h such that every n>=N_delta and every h>=0 obey
W_n(h)>=(1-delta)U_n^ZJ. Zeros, arbitrary large steps and all horizons remain.

Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2,

C_n=max_(k+l=n-1)Omega(C_k,C_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(C_k,B_l),
over ALL integer splits, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- A lower witness above U for one schedule is neither a minimax disproof
  nor an all-word affirmative theorem.
- A complete audit is not verifier acceptance. General component drafts
  are not promoted wholesale by an accepted specialization.
- A bound on a reduced mass channel is not a bound on its actual function;
  the inequality direction and closest-minimizer normalization matter.
- The single-source-stage radius cannot be copied into a longer chain.
  Nor can actual backward P,Q be preserved by replacing a block by its sum.
- Small turning angle reduces a LOCAL allowance, but remote old-plane
  constraints, metric conditioning and any sum smoothness remain payable.
- Averaging illegal transcripts, resetting old values, or repairing only
  adjacent interpolation inequalities cannot define the needed function.
- A positive tangent/Gram direction is not full nonlinear feasibility;
  a local capacity bound is not a global or objective-minimax bound.
- Finite-field patterns or floating-point positive eigenvalues cannot
  replace ordered-real sign and PSD proofs in the actual certificate.
- Do not delete independent formal coordinates at zero physical steps,
  assume arbitrary-template inflation, or conflate enclosing-m families.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

**Bridge1, actual sufficient nonzero-PSD seed.** Produce one complete
original feasible array at a sufficient capacity, outside the locally
excluded region. A symbolic real-algebraic or SOS construction must retain
every sign, exact flow and the actual full remainder. Existing accepted
physical lifts then apply. A restricted parameter-family obstruction must
state only that restricted scope.

finite calibration to an unbounded stage count using one static function,
keeping every mixture, positive proximal height and original query. Prove
all remote constraints and true initial distance and compute the complete
accumulated loss. A local arc or pairwise transfer formula is insufficient.

**Bridge3, true-radius arbitrary-count comparison.** On the SAME small-tau
H_(m,tau), control actual ordered P_i,Q_i, all transfer products and the
full source minimum polyhedron for every selection; alternatively produce
a genuine normalized escape with actual trajectory and unbounded depth.
The zero/one-checkpoint theorem and wide scalar collapse are inputs for
orientation, not the missing all-count inequality.

**Bridge4, uniform profile matching.** Given a genuinely legal all-word
family, establish a single word-independent vanishing loss at the exact
all-split B_n scale for every sufficiently large n. This bridge remains
separate even if any diagnostic family or geometric sublemma is closed.

starts are weak. They are not all-depth upper bounds. Likewise the finite

certificate or capacity maximum;its diagnostic eigenvalue stop proves no
infeasibility. The log-cosh chain's weak untouched-coordinate LOWER
certificate is not an upper bound on actual chain error.

# 2. Interface contract table

### Full seed to actual asymptotic separation
**Input required.** Every original seed hypothesis in Section0,plus a proved
strict cover m^K b0<=2^ell d and A^K>lambda^ell C_pc,where m=r+1 and
Z_d/Z_b0<=C_pc. All b0,d,K are positive integers and ell>=0 is an integer.
**Output claimed.**5e1848ee0b153f5f fixes a finite selected phase b_i=b0 m^i
and true physical horizons N_(i,j)=m b_i 2^j-m. For certified a,gamma with
1<a<=A,a^K>lambda^ell C_pc,K gamma<=1-lambda^ell C_pc/a^K,
all j>=ceil(log(60 b0 m^(K-1)/gamma)/log(3/2)) satisfy
W_N<(1-gamma/4)U_N^ZJ. No phase oracle or efficiency claim is used.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750,4c92d3fc4eb6153e,5e1848ee0b153f5f.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** An actual seed and full array
at a sufficient capacity;the conditional theorem itself is accepted.
**Failure mode if ignored.** An input-existence problem is silently erased.
**Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### PSD coordinates to original coefficient feasibility
**Input required.** Original T,L,D,flow,all signs,full formal gradient space;
zero,unit,subunit and singular cases remain explicit.
**Output claimed.**The unverified chart gives PSD-built-in coordinates and
skew reconstruction without an asserted lossless support/rank restriction.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 provides the underlying identity.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The chart as a theorem,and an
actual reconstructed nonnegative array with sufficient capacity.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Real equality feasibility is confused with
original sign feasibility. The new seven-step static obstructions show why
positive unit moment and all global cuts are insufficient.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Static nonlinear law to useful unbounded terminal error
**Input required.** One actual static convex L-smooth function per word,
full chronological queries,attained minimum,true initial distance and every
directed interpolation row,including remote and terminal pairs.
**Output claimed.**A quantitative lower payoff at original B_n scale.
**Available facts.**1252b8823bb5a290 and the complete scoped reference facts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A useful terminal estimate
for a concrete unbounded family. Legality,noncommuting Hessians or a small
graph spectral gap by themselves do not supply it.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A lawful but rapidly decaying function is
mistaken for a hard instance,or an averaged illegal oracle replaces a function.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Diagnostic family to the original affirmative quantifiers
**Input required.** An actual lower construction for EVERY n and EVERY h>=0,
with a relative loss tending to zero uniformly in the word.
**Output claimed.**The exact uniform affirmative statement of Section0.
**Available facts.**Only generic interpolation and restricted witnesses.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** The entire all-word extension,
even after a hypothetical successful H_m family result.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A dyadic or schedule-dependent estimate is
substituted for the requested horizon-by-horizon minimax profile.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "A>(r+1)^rho is impossible by the exponent theorem."
  **Conditional only.** The accepted result gives a sufficient interface.
  An arbitrary-template recursive certificate is still absent;the power
  A^K in the proof is a telescoping ratio product,not a new physical seed.
- "The full PSD image is a primitive certificate."

  are rejected by actual nonlinear static functions at draft level.

  staircase/sector hypotheses.62a7d9329121fd75 compares only its named channels.
- "A vanishing spectral gap gives the needed lower error."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.** A chronological nonlinear trajectory,
  sector transitions,true radius and terminal energy still need proof.
- "Finite weak trajectories refute eventual usefulness."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.** Finite floating-point or higher-precision
  agreement does not supply an all-depth analytic bound.

Do not delete zero-step formal coordinates in primitive certificates.
Do not restart nonlinear child state or discard old function-value areas.
Do not transfer the old c=13/5,no-bridge-plus-one scalar word to original H_m.
Do not reweight the closed triangle-identity family and call it a new geometry.
Do not infer a global seed-capacity theorem from a local/two-path obstruction.
Do not replace the exact all-split benchmark or W=2R normalization.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1 - actual sufficient full primitive seed
**Target statement.**The exact existential statement of Section0,with all
original signs,flow and full PSD;in particular r5,A>=49/5 or r6,A>=12 suffices.
For r7 the accepted general interface is A>8^rho=lambda^3.
**Prerequisites.**One actual word and complete coefficient array.
**Existing facts to use.**04219554e41d9750 and5e1848ee0b153f5f.
**Missing checks.** Actual exact feasibility at sufficient capacity.
**Closure criterion.** A joint exact object survives independent attack and
acceptance,with all hypotheses matched to the physical lift. Independent
of the lower-function route. Neither two path exclusions nor tangent
directions alone satisfy this existence obligation.

### Bridge2 - actual terminal energy of the fixed sparse directed cycle
**Target statement.**For integer m>=8,set lambda=1+sqrt2,beta=1-1/m,
c=lambda(1+1/m),a=beta/c,t_j=1+a c^j,H0 empty,
H_(j+1)=(H_j,t_j,H_j),j<m,with the SAME enclosing m in all children.

v_i=cos((2i+1)theta),F(x)=sum_i(x_i-x_(i+1 mod N))_+^2/8.
Run the actual original word from x0=v with L=1 and true R^2=N/2.
In edge coordinates y=Dx use exactly
E_t(y)_i=y_i-(t/4)(2(y_i)_+-(y_(i-1))_+-(y_(i+1))_+).
Define Psi0=identity,Psi_(j+1)=Psi_j o E_(t_j) o Psi_j,and
J_m=N||[Psi_(m-1)(-Dv)]_+||^2.
The target is a quantitative actual asymptotic estimate on J_m,sufficient
to establish a useful original-B lower score or an actual family upper deficit.

**Prerequisites.**The fixed static function,all directed rows,actual
chronology,attained minimum and true radius in draft37ec6ea9a52bfbdf must
be justified jointly. Its first-child scalar amplitude is
A_m=product_(j=0)^(m-2)(1-mu t_j)^(2^(m-2-j)),mu=sin(theta)^2,
and b_m=A_m(mu d-1). The draft's exact terminal formula is
V_m=b_m^2 J_m/(2N^2).
It claims b_m->A_infty/2>0,where
A_infty=product_(r=1)^infinity(1-(3/2)lambda^(-r))^(2^(r-1)),and
B_(2^m-1)V_m=K_m J_m with
K_m->K_cycle=3lambda^2 A_infty^2 Z1/(4pi^2 exp(1))>0.
These are unverified joint-draft inputs,not new accepted facts.

**Existing facts to use.**1252b8823bb5a290 supplies full directed
interpolation;04219554e41d9750 supplies the unconditional integer ray Z1.
**Missing checks.**The nonlinear RIGHT-child energy estimate,including
actual large steps,sign cancellations,ties and changing active paths.
A limiting comparison at least1 would require liminf J_m>=1/K_cycle;
a positive but insufficient constant would not match the benchmark.
Conversely an actual eventual upper deficit could screen this chosen law
without necessarily proving J_m tends to zero.
**Closure criterion.**A readable uniform actual-terminal estimate with all
preliminary hypotheses matched. A first-child product,midpoint peak,small
radial gap,numerical trend or formal recurrence alone does not close it.
This is independent of Bridge1,and even a success leaves Bridge3 open.
### Bridge3 - uniform arbitrary-word extension
**Target statement.**For every epsilon>0,there exists N_epsilon such that
for every n>=N_epsilon and h in R_+^n,one actual legal finite-dimensional
instance has normalized error at least(1-epsilon)/B_n.
**Prerequisites.**A concrete construction whose loss is controlled uniformly,
including zeros,arbitrary large steps and chronological ordering.
**Existing facts to use.**1252b8823bb5a290 and only scope-matched witnesses.
**Missing checks.** The construction and the uniform error bound themselves.
**Closure criterion.** This exact target-strength bridge needs a real new
mechanism;renaming the minimax inequality a Bellman lemma is not progress.
It is logically separate from an H_m diagnostic success and from Bridge1.

# 2. Interface contract table

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1 - actual sufficient full primitive seed
**Target statement.**The exact existential statement of Section0,with all
original signs,flow and full PSD;in particular r5,A>=49/5 or r6,A>=12 suffices.
For r7 the accepted general interface is A>8^rho=lambda^3.

### Bridge2 - actual terminal energy of the fixed sparse directed cycle
**Target statement.**For integer m>=8,set lambda=1+sqrt2,beta=1-1/m,
c=lambda(1+1/m),a=beta/c,t_j=1+a c^j,H0 empty,
H_(j+1)=(H_j,t_j,H_j),j<m,with the SAME enclosing m in all children.

v_i=cos((2i+1)theta),F(x)=sum_i(x_i-x_(i+1 mod N))_+^2/8.
Run the actual original word from x0=v with L=1 and true R^2=N/2.
In edge coordinates y=Dx use exactly
E_t(y)_i=y_i-(t/4)(2(y_i)_+-(y_(i-1))_+-(y_(i+1))_+).
Define Psi0=identity,Psi_(j+1)=Psi_j o E_(t_j) o Psi_j,and
J_m=N||[Psi_(m-1)(-Dv)]_+||^2.
The target is a quantitative actual asymptotic estimate on J_m,sufficient
to establish a useful original-B lower score or an actual family upper deficit.

**Prerequisites.**The fixed static function,all directed rows,actual
chronology,attained minimum and true radius in draft37ec6ea9a52bfbdf must
be justified jointly. Its first-child scalar amplitude is
A_m=product_(j=0)^(m-2)(1-mu t_j)^(2^(m-2-j)),mu=sin(theta)^2,
and b_m=A_m(mu d-1). The draft's exact terminal formula is
V_m=b_m^2 J_m/(2N^2).
It claims b_m->A_infty/2>0,where
A_infty=product_(r=1)^infinity(1-(3/2)lambda^(-r))^(2^(r-1)),and
B_(2^m-1)V_m=K_m J_m with
K_m->K_cycle=3lambda^2 A_infty^2 Z1/(4pi^2 exp(1))>0.
These are unverified joint-draft inputs,not new accepted facts.

**Existing facts to use.**1252b8823bb5a290 supplies full directed
interpolation;04219554e41d9750 supplies the unconditional integer ray Z1.
**Missing checks.**The nonlinear RIGHT-child energy estimate,including
actual large steps,sign cancellations,ties and changing active paths.
A limiting comparison at least1 would require liminf J_m>=1/K_cycle;
a positive but insufficient constant would not match the benchmark.

without necessarily proving J_m tends to zero.

### Bridge3 - uniform arbitrary-word extension
**Target statement.**For every epsilon>0,there exists N_epsilon such that
for every n>=N_epsilon and h in R_+^n,one actual legal finite-dimensional
instance has normalized error at least(1-epsilon)/B_n.

### Bridge4 - distinguish first-order separation from an actual local bound
**Target statement.**At the exact length7 word
hbar=(sqrt2,2,sqrt2,2+sqrt2,sqrt2,2,sqrt2),prove or refute existence of
epsilon>0 such that EVERY h>=0 with ||h-hbar||<epsilon and ANY original

on unbounded physical horizons with a fixed-relative benchmark margin.

and every nonnegative word, with a word-independent asymptotic remainder.

# Typed-layout screens and the full continuation interface

2026-10-02. SELF-AUTHORED main synthesis; consult OFF. Goal ACTIVE,
original target OPEN. Model gpt-6.1-sol, reasoning max.

## Verdict and unchanged target

Fourteen scoped accepted facts, no accepted original-target proof.
For predetermined h>=0, actual GD has W_n(h)=sup
2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2), over every finite dimension,
convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, and x0!=x_star.
The original target is W_n^*/U_n^ZJ ->1, W=2R, with
U_n^ZJ=1/B_n and the original ALL-split benchmark. An affirmative
proof needs every sufficiently large horizon and every word with a
word-independent vanishing relative error. A negative proof needs an
actual full-function-class upper improvement by a fixed relative gap on
unbounded physical horizons. Restricted examples and method failures
do not meet either condition.

## New full shared evidence, all UNVERIFIED

The full max3r66 report 488a49d87beacf62 was read. Its unequal two-block
families, with or without a leading basic objective block, fail for
every specified nonnegative bridge/recovery coefficient. A specified
primitive-objective-primitive layout has adequate Huber mass but fails
by an actual quadratic plus asymmetric-Huber cut. The objective module
is not primitive. These exclusions apply only to the displayed words.
They are not an arbitrary-template inflation theorem or a capacity ceiling.

The full max4r69 report 20618061fa719178 was read. It supplies a
ratio-dependent three-stage construction with unequal ordinary masses
and an explicit terminal active-weight cap. Its initialized small-ratio
diagnostic is weaker than Huber. A separate actual p-q-r-q law with first
ratio1/2 has complete eight-anchor terminal evaluation, minimum/radius,
and a growing prescribed-family gain over K, quadratics, the universal
lens, and its precisely defined homogeneous two-block/last-cut policy.
It does not cover arbitrary words or all other adversarial policies.

The same report excludes affine-height repair of its named fixed-two-plane
q-r-q return when D*C<M2*M3. This includes its FIRST-OCCURRENCE hierarchy
partition; it does not exclude other checkpoint positions, changing
gradients on an edge, more faces, or general static smooth functions.

## Correctness inputs and dangerous transfers

Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies full directed interpolation and maximal
unqueried-terminal completion while retaining every old jet. Accepted
b98bb807d514e80d supplies the actual Huber/all-cut channels and exact
benchmark definitions. Accepted04219554e41d9750 supplies exact typed
module identities and unconditional original benchmark enclosures.
Accepteddfde2fa326c908bf supplies the named complex/polynomial channel,
not an unrestricted nonlinear upper bound. Accepted5fd396eba75651e7

Do not assume an objective block is primitive, or treat an entire block
as one gradient oracle step. Do not use a mass advantage as a full upper
certificate. Do not import an UNVERIFIED local geometry as an accepted
brick: restate and jointly prove any new use. Keep the terminal cap.
Do not infer that independent intercepts remove an active-plane debt.
Do not infer a full run from a truncated prefix, a terminal handoff from
an exit, or original minimax completion from a special-family fact.

V(h) with W_n(h)>=V(h); (2) EVERYepsilon>0,one N_epsilon independent of h,
ALLn>=N_epsilon/ALLh>=0, V(h)>=(1-epsilon)/B_n,or a correctly scoped combined
portfolio comparison. The second is a theorem-level missing bridge until
proved. Legal construction,positive value,finite family or exponent alone
cannot close the Goal. A decisive failure on an actual diagnostic can close
only the specified selector/template,not W_n itself.

Use SAME-enclosing-m tau1/4 H_m and the accepted c3bfc special words as
diagnostics when relevant; never substitute one word's parameters/benchmark
for another. A selector pointwise below known K adds no lower channel.
A positive witness at a few computed horizons is not uniform profile evidence.

cap1,up to3 explicit selector laws or precise whole-template obstructions.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is exactly lim_n W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a rigorous disproof. Here W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||^2), over all finite dimensions, convex differentiable L-smooth functions with attained minima, and the original GD updates for every predetermined nonnegative word. W=2R. An affirmative result requires every epsilon>0, one N_epsilon independent of h, every n>=N_epsilon and every h>=0. A negative result requires a fixed relative separation on unbounded actual horizons for the FULL function class.

Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies original ordered interpolation and actual unqueried-terminal completion. Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 supplies the all-integer-split benchmark envelope and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50. The credible conditional negative architecture is a full original-space certificate W_n(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), followed by that accepted comparison. Its central inequality is still missing. Current failures are method failure, not proposition failure. PARTIAL or SUBSTANTIAL labels do not mean that the unrestricted problem is nearly solved.

# 2. Interface contract table

### Original full-space certificate
Input required: every original Q_ij<=0, value cancellation, nonnegative multipliers, and PSD on independent x_0,g_0,...,g_n, with the initial charge paid once. Output claimed: W_n(h)<=q. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 gives the row orientation and terminal completion. Missing verification on the actual model: explicit coefficient law with all finite and optimum signs at q<=17/(16D_m). Failure mode if ignored: a signed factor can pass PSD while proving no upper bound. Status:PARTIAL.

### Separate terminal and common directions
Input required: original conjugate coordinates, explicit probabilities, independent common column z with e^Tz=p+s, explicit actual skew divergence and paid zero-sum imbalance, all original signs. Output claimed: a potential extension of the restricted matching architecture. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 only for original row algebra; the displayed extension in research/INDEPENDENT_COMMON_SQUARE_ADVISORY_20261003.md is not accepted. Missing verification: the joint full identity, global load/row moments and an actual sign-feasible law. Failure mode: treating a free common square as free transport or assuming its PSD implies interpolation signs. Status:PARTIAL.

### Signed scalar energy
Input required: one static clipped derivative with independent positive curvatures, all starts, exact thresholds and actual same-m children. Output claimed: a quantitative family objective profile or improving legal witness. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 only compares a subsequently proved bound with the benchmark. Missing verification: independent-curvature multiple crossings while retaining destination energy and caps. Failure mode: a weak curvature permits a large radius that is not a large objective. Status:PARTIAL.

### Full triangle face changes
Input required: the centered equilateral body, all initial positions and all original face guards, with the actual Euclidean denominator. Output claimed: a uniform quantitative profile or an explicit all-depth legal selector. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 supplies scalar benchmark comparison only. Missing verification: change-of-face recursion with tangential terms and simultaneous guards. Failure mode: optimizing incompatible children, discarding a transverse coordinate, or selecting a finite expression without bounding it. Status:PARTIAL.

### Quantitative conclusion in the original model
Input required: a full-class upper bound on the displayed H_m or a uniform all-word lower bound with the exact benchmark. Output claimed: disproof by fixed separation, or the requested limit. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 closes the negative scalar comparison after the missing hypothesis; b98bb807d514e80d fixes the positive normalization. Missing verification: the quantitative full-class inequality. Failure mode: restricted bodies/functions and finitely many horizons do not cover the goal. Status:SUBSTANTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- DANGEROUS: 'Full PSD and optimum signs are enough.' Original finite row signs are a separate requirement;121bbbe4b6a46fe3 isolates a first-row clock obstruction after all those screens pass.
- DANGEROUS: 'The larger curvature controls every radius.' The explicit clipped counterexample in7476ebfe7be45713 retains radius while paying small objective curvature. A correct energy statement must retain the terminal side.
- DANGEROUS: 'Every fixed face is Huber-optimal, so changing faces are too.' Actual projection maps are nonlinear across guards and tangential motion survives a bridge.
- DANGEROUS: 'Degree at most8 means the uniform triangle maximum is known.' The maximizing face history still ranges over the full original word.
- DANGEROUS: 'Simplex representation supplies a schedule-only lower witness.' The representation starts from an already chosen run, with a full polar minimum cone.
- DANGEROUS: 'A failed formal PSD direction is a convex-smooth counterexample.' All original interpolation rows still need to hold for a single static function.

Concrete anti-routes: fixing a known impossible load law while retuning only balanced skew; replacing independent curvature by its maximum in a radial lemma; dropping the last leaf; resetting child m; propagating a convex hull through a nonlinear map without justification; calling scoped family results full W bounds. These choices lose identified load-bearing constraints.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1: explicit common-direction and load matching
Target statement: for every same-m H_m above, construct actual original-row nonnegative coefficients with value cancellation and a complete independent-vector PSD factor at17/(16D_m). Prerequisites: explicit finite formulas for all weights and one globally paid initial charge. Existing facts:1252b8823bb5a290,931b9653ce0f7201 at stated scopes. Missing checks: common/terminal coupling, incoming and outgoing optimum loads, all finite row signs and objective level. Closure criterion: all original signs and the full factor hold at all m>=1536, with no feasible-matrix oracle. Independent of the diagnostic family branches.

### Bridge2: independent-curvature terminal energies
Target statement: for every positive b_plus,b_minus, positive kappa_plus,kappa_minus<=1 and real start s, establish the full same-m score <=K/(1+2KS), K=max curvatures, or construct an explicit full-word family violating this candidate with exact payoff. Prerequisites: one global clipped function and actual threshold/sign history. Existing facts: original normalization b98bb807d514e80d; benchmark comparison931b9653ce0f7201. Missing checks: all repeated returns with source/destination energy ratios and genuine tails. Closure criterion: all independent parameter ranges and starts, not an unevaluated recurrence. Independent; a success still covers only the stated family.

### Bridge3: changing-face triangle profile
Target statement: for D=conv{(1,0),(-1/2,sqrt3/2),(-1/2,-sqrt3/2)}, F_D(x)=max_(g in D)(x.g-||g||^2/2), obtain a uniform bound on sup_(x_0!=0)2F_D(Psi_m(x_0))/||x_0||^2 or an explicit all-depth witness. Prerequisites: all actual projection guards, coordinates and the true radius. Existing facts:931b9653ce0f7201 for a later comparison only. Missing checks: changing supports, compatible child states and tangential energies. Closure criterion: quantified all-m objective bound or legal selector; finite algebraic enumeration alone is insufficient. Independent; no automatic extension to arbitrary dimension.

A distinct physical occupation-barycenter terminal rule is refuted by the legal quadratic curvature2/T_root: its surrogate is bounded below by exp(-260), while the actual objective is below1/(16D_m) and an available original post-root anchor bounds the full functional below1/(4D_m). Thus relative anchor geometry, not another scalar mixing parameter, is the missing interface. All these new claims remain JOINT UNVERIFIED.

# 2. Interface contract table

### Original full-space certificate
Input required: every original Q_ij<=0, value cancellation, nonnegative multipliers, and PSD on independent x_0,g_0,...,g_n, with the initial charge paid once. Output claimed: W_n(h)<=q. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 gives the row orientation and terminal completion. Missing verification on the actual model: explicit coefficient law with all finite and optimum signs at q<=17/(16D_m). Failure mode if ignored: a signed factor can pass PSD while proving no upper bound. Status:PARTIAL.

### Separate terminal and common directions
Input required: original conjugate coordinates, explicit probabilities, independent common column z with e^Tz=p+s, explicit actual skew divergence and paid zero-sum imbalance, all original signs. Output claimed: a potential extension of the restricted matching architecture. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 only for original row algebra; the displayed extension in research/INDEPENDENT_COMMON_SQUARE_ADVISORY_20261003.md is not accepted. Missing verification: the joint full identity, global load/row moments and an actual sign-feasible law. Failure mode: treating a free common square as free transport or assuming its PSD implies interpolation signs. Status:PARTIAL.

### Signed scalar energy
Input required: one static clipped derivative with independent positive curvatures, all starts, exact thresholds and actual same-m children. Output claimed: a quantitative family objective profile or improving legal witness. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 only compares a subsequently proved bound with the benchmark. Missing verification: independent-curvature multiple crossings while retaining destination energy and caps. Failure mode: a weak curvature permits a large radius that is not a large objective. Status:PARTIAL.

### Full triangle face changes
Input required: the centered equilateral body, all initial positions and all original face guards, with the actual Euclidean denominator. Output claimed: a uniform quantitative profile or an explicit all-depth legal selector. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 supplies scalar benchmark comparison only. Missing verification: change-of-face recursion with tangential terms and simultaneous guards. Failure mode: optimizing incompatible children, discarding a transverse coordinate, or selecting a finite expression without bounding it. Status:PARTIAL.

### Quantitative conclusion in the original model
Input required: a full-class upper bound on the displayed H_m or a uniform all-word lower bound with the exact benchmark. Output claimed: disproof by fixed separation, or the requested limit. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 closes the negative scalar comparison after the missing hypothesis; b98bb807d514e80d fixes the positive normalization. Missing verification: the quantitative full-class inequality. Failure mode: restricted bodies/functions and finitely many horizons do not cover the goal. Status:SUBSTANTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- DANGEROUS: 'Full PSD and optimum signs are enough.' Original finite row signs are a separate requirement;121bbbe4b6a46fe3 isolates a first-row clock obstruction after all those screens pass.
- DANGEROUS: 'The larger curvature controls every radius.' The explicit clipped counterexample in7476ebfe7be45713 retains radius while paying small objective curvature. A correct energy statement must retain the terminal side.
- DANGEROUS: 'Every fixed face is Huber-optimal, so changing faces are too.' Actual projection maps are nonlinear across guards and tangential motion survives a bridge.
- DANGEROUS: 'Degree at most8 means the uniform triangle maximum is known.' The maximizing face history still ranges over the full original word.
- DANGEROUS: 'Simplex representation supplies a schedule-only lower witness.' The representation starts from an already chosen run, with a full polar minimum cone.
- DANGEROUS: 'A failed formal PSD direction is a convex-smooth counterexample.' All original interpolation rows still need to hold for a single static function.

Concrete anti-routes: fixing a known impossible load law while retuning only balanced skew; replacing independent curvature by its maximum in a radial lemma; dropping the last leaf; resetting child m; propagating a convex hull through a nonlinear map without justification; calling scoped family results full W bounds. These choices lose identified load-bearing constraints.

### Bridge3: changing-face triangle profile

Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 closes the negative scalar comparison after the missing hypothesis; b98bb807d514e80d fixes the positive normalization. Missing verification: the quantitative full-class inequality. Failure mode: restricted bodies/functions and finitely many horizons do not cover the goal. Status:SUBSTANTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- DANGEROUS: 'Full PSD and optimum signs are enough.' Original finite row signs are a separate requirement;121bbbe4b6a46fe3 isolates a first-row clock obstruction after all those screens pass.
- DANGEROUS: 'The larger curvature controls every radius.' The explicit clipped counterexample in7476ebfe7be45713 retains radius while paying small objective curvature. A correct energy statement must retain the terminal side.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

NOT SOLVED. The original target remains the exact every-horizon, every-nonnegative-word limit W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a rigorous full-function asymptotic separation. W=2R. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 proves the all-split benchmark bound and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50. A FULL-class upper W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) would yield a fixed separation; that inequality remains of theorem-level strength and is not assumed. Accepted d8b59c7d92425121 represents each finite-word full supremum by affine-height envelopes over at most n+2 polytope vertices, with true initial radius and an orthogonal height vector. It is a structural equality, not a quantitative rate.

The quantitative proof skeleton still requires either an explicit original-space nonnegative interpolation certificate with a paid PSD remainder, or a uniform legal adversary for every word. The current explicit certificate architecture and the scalar/geometric diagnostic routes are independent. None closes the original target.

|Finite exposed-segment diagnostic|JOINT UNVERIFIED|Complete subclass supremum 1/D, report 5960de36af8e17d5|Independent audit and genuine multi-face extension|

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.

The following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.

New joint report870832ac79589e74 gives a complete arbitrary-body actual H3 U identity, using the actual maximizer at the right child's input. Both H2 primitive reserves and every physical/auxiliary normal are retained. The natural U-only child join is refuted by the fixed interval[-1,1], children3/2 and bridge9/4, with ratio575/512 at trailing d1. Higher-depth propagation needs actual remainder transfer, not just child inequality signs. New joint reporta0925e826bbc6304 covers every positive first inner-reversing level whose initial H_J ends in the core, with full-word score<4/(5D); all starts R<=v(c-1)/(c-2) meet this gate. The exterior-source complement remains open. A coarse constant gradient bound, together with a proved pre-cap terminal and original curvature, is a new sufficient interface distinct from the false exact amplitude lemma. These are UNVERIFIED sources. The earlier gradient-projection simplex universality candidate is undergoing independent audit; it is not yet an accepted fact.

# 2. Interface contracts

Projection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.

Kink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.

Multi-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.

# 4. Missing bridges

First, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.

Accepted fact931b9653ce0f7201 establishes C_n<=(n+1)^rho, B_n<=(12/5)(n+1)^rho for all integer splits, and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50 for the SAME-enclosing-m H_(m,1/4), every m>=1536. # 3. Dangerous shortcuts

Do not turn an E80-feasible transcript into a function: its named missing row is violated. Do not treat a symmetric row pair or circulation as PSD; its displacement term is indefinite. Do not preserve a failed contact equation while spending objective slack. Do not infer whole-space positivity from finitely many modes or a temporal quotient.

Do not identify Walsh orthogonality or expected Gram matrices with one real trajectory. A diagonal anisotropy is not an innocuous coordinate change under Euclidean GD. Pay every carrier, extreme coordinate, positive conjugate height and true initial radius. Do not assume identical coordinates split spontaneously.

Do not export a symmetric-Huber radial argument to asymmetric or scaled curvatures without proving the changed envelope. Do not optimize child start points independently. Do not reset enclosing-m parameters at subtrees. Do not identify a three-sector endpoint certificate with full independent-gradient PSD.

Every benchmark comparison uses ALL integer splits; the UPPER bound B_n/D_m<47/50 cannot establish a lower witness's gain by reversal. No finite scan, exact recurrence alone, exponent-only result, conditional matrix inequality or restricted-family bound completes the original target.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

The exact full-space Schur condition and its all-depth coefficients must be proved, not posed as an unknown SDP. Accepted931 supplies only the final scalar comparison once such a bound exists.
